### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPとUDPの一番の違いは、TCPは届いたことと順番を保証する（信頼性重視）のに対し、UDPは保証せず速く軽く送る（速度重視）ことです。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa **rebase** para ordenar tu rama privada, sobre todo antes de abrir un PR, si quieres historial lineal y aún no la has compartido. Usa **merge** cuando otros ya trabajan sobre la rama o quieres conservar tal cual cuándo y cómo se integraron los cambios.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 可靠（保证送达、按序、出错重传，但有连接建立和确认的开销），UDP 不可靠但快（无连接、不保证送达和顺序，开销小）。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unshared branch to tidy its commits or catch up with main before you open a PR. Merge when the branch is already shared with others, or when you want the history to show exactly when the branches came together.
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to a database before that change is applied to the data itself. If the system crashes midway, it replays the log on restart to recover committed work and discard anything half-finished.
```

```
要做的：
- 按 review 里提的意见，把 compaction 相关的锁粒度改细（缩小持锁范围或拆成更细的锁），不改动无关逻辑。
- 改完跑相关测试，把结果作为证据报告回来（测试命令和通过情况）。
- 改动提交在这个任务自己的分支上。

完成标准：review 提的锁粒度问题都处理了；相关测试通过；报告里写明改了哪些锁、怎么改的。

不在范围内：不推送、不合并、不改 PR 的状态（草稿保持原样）。推送前先告诉我，等我点头。

地点：沿用这个任务原来的仓库和分支。
```

```
  [an app card under the message: made "宝宝喝奶", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 3595 characters): <style>
.k-row{cursor:pointer}
</style>
<main class="k-app">
  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">今天喝奶</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="total"></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="count"></div>
  </section>
  <section class="k-card">
    <div class="k-sum-label">距上次</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="since"></div>
  </section>
  <h2 class="k-h">今天的记录</h2>
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty">今天还没有记录,在下面记一笔</div>
  <details class="k-add" id="form">
    <summary>记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-field"><label>时间</label><input class="k-input" id="t" placeholder="HH:MM"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label>奶量 (ml)</label><input class="k-num" id="ml" inputmode="decimal"></div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">记下</button>
    <button class="k-btn" id="del" hidden>删除这条</button>
  </details>
</main>
<script>
let feeds = [];
let editIdx = -1;
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const pad = n => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
const dayKey = d => d.getFullYear() + '-' + pad(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + pad(d.getDate());
const nowHM = () => { const d = new Date(); return pad(d.getHours()) + ':' + pad(d.getMinutes());  … ne">还没记</span>';
    $('count').textContent = '今天还没有记录';
    $('since').textContent = '—';
    $('empty').hidden = false;
    ducter.summary('今天还没记');
  }
}

function reset() {
  editIdx = -1;
  $('t').value = nowHM();
  $('ml').value = '';
  $('del').hidden = true;
  $('form').open = false;
}

function edit(i) {
  editIdx = i;
  const f = feeds[i];
  $('t').value = f.at.slice(11);
  $('ml').value = f.ml;
  $('del').hidden = false;
  $('form').open = true;
}

function save() { ducter.set('feeds', { feeds: feeds }); render(); }

$('save').onclick = () => {
  const t = $('t').value.trim();
  const ml = parseFloat($('ml').value);
  if (!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(t) || !(ml > 0)) return;
  const [h, m] = t.split(':').map(Number);
  const rec = { at: dayKey(new Date()) + ' ' + pad(h) + ':' + pad(m), ml: ml };
  if (editIdx >= 0) feeds[editIdx] = rec; else feeds.push(rec);
  save();
  reset();
};

$('del').onclick = () => {
  if (editIdx >= 0) feeds.splice(editIdx, 1);
  save();
  reset();
};

function load() {
  const v = ducter.get('feeds');
  feeds = (v && Array.isArray(v.feeds)) ? v.feeds : [];
  render();
}

reset();
load();
ducter.onChange(load);
setInterval(render, 60000);
</script>
; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"at":"2026-11-28 06:10","ml":120},{"at":"2026-11-28 09:05","ml":90}]}
```

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: the page is on your shelf with the four PRs, and you can tick each one off when it lands. The statuses are as of 20:41 today, so they won't update on their own.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on others", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2220 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="left">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">PRs open as of Sat 20:41 · tap one when it lands</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Open PRs</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var items = ducter.get("items") || [];
  var landed = {};

  function save() { try { ducter.set("landed", landed); } catch (e) {} }

  function render() {
    var list = document.getElementById("list");
    list.innerHTML = "";
    var open = 0;
    items.forEach(function (it) {
      var done = !!landed[it.id];
      if (!done) open++;
      var row = document.createElement("button");
      row.className = "k-row";
      row.setAttribute("aria-pressed", done ? "true" : "false");
      row.style.width = "100%";
      row.style.background = "none";
      row.style.border = "0";
      row.style.textAlign = "left";
      row.style.font = "inherit";
      row.style.color = "inherit";
      row.style.cursor = "pointer";
      row.innerHTML =
        '<span class="k-row-main" style="' + (done ? "text-decoration:line-through;color:var(--d-muted)" : "") + '">' +
        (done ? "☑ " : "☐ ") + it.title + "</span>" +
        '<span class="k-row-sub">' + (done ? "Landed" : it.sub) + "</span>";
      row.addEventListener("click", function () {
        landed[it.id] = !landed[it.id];
        save();
        render();
      });
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    document.getElementById("left").textContent = String(open);
    document.getElementById("sub").textContent = open
      ? "PRs still open · tap one when it lands"
      : "All landed";
    try { ducter.summary(open + " PRs waiting, " + (items.length - open) + " landed"); } catch (e) {}
  }

  try {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get("landed")).then(function (v) {
      if (v && typeof v === "object") landed = v;
      render();
    });
    ducter.onChange(function () {
      Promise.resolve(ducter.get("landed")).then(function (v) {
        landed = (v && typeof v === "object") ? v : {};
        render();
      });
    });
  } catch (e) { render(); }
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"wey-gu/ducter #412 · compaction cut","sub":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"nowledge-co/mem #10287 · retry on 502","sub":"CI running, currently failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"nowledge-co/hawdb #1592 · flaky compaction test","sub":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"wey-gu/ducter #418 · apps bar","sub":"Draft, not yet ready for review"}]}

```

```
  [an app card under the message: changed "出差花销", with Open] the app's file now (v2, 2946 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum"><div class="k-sum-label">出差花销合计</div><div class="k-sum-value" id="total"></div><div class="k-sum-sub" id="count"></div></div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary>
<div class="k-card">
<div class="k-field"><label for="trip">出差名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip" placeholder="如 北京出差 11/20–21"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label for="day">日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="如 2026-11-20"></div>
<div class="k-chips" id="cats"><button class="k-chip" data-c="交通" aria-pressed="true">交通</button><button class="k-chip" data-c="住宿" aria-pressed="false">住宿</button></div>
<div class="k-field"><label for="amt">金额</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal"></div>
<button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
</div>
</details>
</main>
<script>
var cat = "交通";
function money(n){ return "¥" + Number(n).toLocaleString("en-US"); }
function el(tag, cls, text){ var e = document.createElement(tag); if(cls) e.className = cls; if(text != null) e.textContent = text; return e; }
function draw(){
  var xs = ducter.get("items") ||  … countEl.textContent = xs.length + " 笔";
    xs.forEach(function(x){
      var row = el("div", "k-row");
      var main = el("div", "k-row-main", x.trip || "出差");
      var sub = el("div", "k-row-sub", [x.day, x.cat, x.note].filter(Boolean).join(" "));
      var val = el("div", "k-row-value", money(x.amt));
      row.appendChild(main); row.appendChild(sub); row.appendChild(val);
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length + " 笔,合计 " + money(total));
}
document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(function(b){
  b.onclick = function(){
    cat = b.getAttribute("data-c");
    document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(function(c){
      c.setAttribute("aria-pressed", c === b ? "true" : "false");
    });
  };
});
document.getElementById("save").onclick = function(){
  var amt = document.getElementById("amt").value;
  if (!amt) return;
  var xs = ducter.get("items") || [];
  xs.push({
    trip: document.getElementById("trip").value || "出差",
    day: document.getElementById("day").value,
    cat: cat,
    amt: Number(amt)
  });
  ducter.set("items", xs);
  document.getElementById("amt").value = "";
  draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"2026-11-20","cat":"交通","amt":662,"note":"G1 07:00 上海虹桥→北京南","from":"你的预订"},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"2026-11-20 至 11-21","cat":"住宿","amt":1036,"note":"亚朵酒店(望京) 两晚","from":"你的预订"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

### wp-thread-unnamed-zh

